const TILE=16, VIEW_W=960, VIEW_H=540, RS=1, F=880;
const WORLD_H=1248, WORLD_W=3200, STAGE_INDEX=0;
const SURFACE_VIEW_BOTTOM=F+7*TILE, HOJA_MAKER_MODE=false;
const WATER_SPANS=[], SOLIDS=[{x:0,y:F,w:WORLD_W,h:WORLD_H-F,kind:'ground'}];
/* V0.4.8 — one canonical four-season draw per INTRO RUN, shared across scenes.
   Begin only when intro starts afresh. Seek/screen navigation never re-rolls. */
window.HOJA_SEASON_SESSION=(()=>{
  const keys=Object.freeze(['spring','summer','autumn','winter']);
  let locked=null,serial=0;
  function begin(){locked=keys[Math.floor(Math.random()*keys.length)];serial++;return locked;}
  function current(){return locked||begin();}
  function restore(key){if(!keys.includes(key))throw new Error('Invalid HOJA season');locked=key;return locked;}
  return {begin,current,restore,get season(){return current();},get runId(){return serial;},keys};
})();
const STAGE={theme:'grassland'};
const THEME={bg:[]};
const INTRO_SEASON_THEMES=Object.freeze({
 spring:{theme:'grassland',bg:[
  {key:'bgSky',xFactor:0,yFactor:0,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'bgCloud1',xFactor:.028,yFactor:.010,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'bgCloud2',xFactor:.055,yFactor:.020,verticalOffset:12},
  {key:'bgHills',xFactor:.100,yFactor:.040,verticalOffset:40},
  {key:'bgForeground',xFactor:.180,yFactor:.070,verticalOffset:42}]},
 summer:{theme:'tropics',bg:[
  {key:'thTroSky',xFactor:0,yFactor:0,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'thTroCloudsBG',xFactor:.035,yFactor:.010,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'thTroClouds',xFactor:.095,yFactor:.030,verticalOffset:20},
  {key:'thTroWaters',xFactor:.18,yFactor:.070,verticalOffset:82}]},
 autumn:{theme:'autumn',bg:[
  {key:'thAutDistant',xFactor:0,yFactor:0,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'thAutRow2',xFactor:.03,yFactor:.010,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'thAutRow1',xFactor:.065,yFactor:.02,verticalOffset:12},
  {key:'thAutPiles',xFactor:.10,yFactor:.040,verticalOffset:35},
  {key:'thAutTrees',xFactor:.14,yFactor:.055,verticalOffset:42},
  {key:'thAutLeafTop',xFactor:.18,yFactor:.07,verticalOffset:42}]},
 winter:{theme:'winter',bg:[
  {key:'thWinMountain',xFactor:0,yFactor:0,verticalOffset:0},
  {key:'thWinHills',xFactor:.075,yFactor:.04,verticalOffset:36},
  {key:'thWinFore',xFactor:.18,yFactor:.07,verticalOffset:42}]}
});
function applyIntroSeason(key){
  const chosen=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(INTRO_SEASON_THEMES,key)?key:window.HOJA_SEASON_SESSION.current();
  const spec=INTRO_SEASON_THEMES[chosen];
  STAGE.theme=spec.theme;
  THEME.bg=spec.bg;
  return chosen;
}

const HOJA_V2_ATLAS_COLS=22;
const HOJA_V2_SURFACE_START={grassland:0,autumn:4,tropics:0,winter:8};
const HOJA_CAVE_AMBIENT_TINT=0xffffff;
function groupFrames(project){

  const groups=new Map(project.groups.map(g=>[g.id,g.name]));

  const out={};

  for(const fr of project.frames){

    const name=groups.get(fr.groupId);

    if(!name)continue;

    (out[name]||(out[name]=[])).push(fr);

  }

  return out;

}

// V0.62 R3.6 EXP: camera zoom/pixel comparison; keep baseline animation rates and original source sprites.

const RAW_GROUPS=groupFrames(PROJECT);



function hexToRGBA(hex){

  if(!hex)return null;

  const n=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16);

  return [(n>>16)&255,(n>>8)&255,n&255,255];

}

const RGBA=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(PROJECT.palette).map(([s,v])=>[s,hexToRGBA(v.color)]));

// R3.6 PIXEL GRID EXPERIMENT: endpoint-only 32x32 nearest-resampled texture.

// This is an in-memory display derivative; the authoritative 64x64 ASCII is unchanged.

function makeHojaPixel32Canvas(frame){

  const src=makeHojaCanvas(frame);

  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=32; c.height=32;

  const g=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});

  g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;

  g.drawImage(src,0,0,64,64,0,0,32,32);

  return c;

}

function makeHojaCanvas(frame){

  const rows=frame.gridRows||String(frame.ascii||'').split('\n');

  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=64;c.height=64;

  const g=c.getContext('2d');g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;

  const im=g.createImageData(64,64),d=im.data;

  for(let y=0;y<64;y++){

    const row=rows[y]||' '.repeat(64);

    for(let x=0;x<64;x++){

      const col=RGBA[row[x]||' '];if(!col)continue;

      const i=(y*64+x)*4;d[i]=col[0];d[i+1]=col[1];d[i+2]=col[2];d[i+3]=255;

    }

  }

  g.putImageData(im,0,0);return c;

}

function applyV06Face(frame,mode){

  const stamp=V06_FACE_STAMPS[String(mode||'').toUpperCase()];

  if(!stamp)throw new Error(`Unknown V0.6 face: ${mode}`);



  const anchor=V06_EXPRESSION_ANCHORS[frame.id];

  if(!anchor)throw new Error(`Missing V0.6 expression anchor: ${frame.id}`);



  const rows=frame.gridRows||String(frame.ascii||'').split('\n');

  const grid=rows.map(r=>r.split(''));

  const x0=anchor.x,y0=anchor.y;



  stamp.forEach((line,i)=>{

    const y=y0+i-2;

    for(let dx=0;dx<line.length;dx++){

      const ch=line[dx];

      if(ch==='-')continue;

      const x=x0+dx;

      if(!grid[y]||!'.EO'.includes(grid[y][x]))continue;

      grid[y][x]=ch;

    }

  });

  return grid.map(r=>r.join(''));

}



function makeHojaExpressionCanvas(frame,mode){

  return makeHojaCanvas({...frame,gridRows:applyV06Face(frame,mode)});

}

class HojaSharedScene extends Phaser.Scene {
  constructor(){super({key:'HojaAttractScene'});}
  applyLightBackground(){/* production LIGHT_BACKGROUND default is false */}

  registerHojaTextures(){
    for(const [group,frames] of Object.entries(RAW_GROUPS)){
      frames.forEach((fr,i)=>{
        const key=`hoja_${group}_${i}`;
        if(!this.textures.exists(key))this.textures.addCanvas(key,makeHojaCanvas(fr));
        if(this.textures.get(key)?.setFilter&&Phaser.Textures?.FilterMode)
          this.textures.get(key).setFilter(Phaser.Textures.FilterMode.NEAREST);
      });
    }
    // R3.7: 64×64 authoring pixels are preserved; no resized textures are registered.
    if(!this.textures.exists('hoja-hurt'))
      this.textures.addCanvas('hoja-hurt',makeHojaExpressionCanvas(RAW_GROUPS.break[0],'hurt'));
    if(!this.textures.exists('hoja-fail'))
      this.textures.addCanvas('hoja-fail',makeHojaExpressionCanvas(RAW_GROUPS.break[0],'fail'));
    if(!this.textures.exists('hoja-clear'))
      this.textures.addCanvas('hoja-clear',makeHojaExpressionCanvas(RAW_GROUPS.IDLE[0],'happy'));

    RAW_GROUPS.IDLE.forEach((fr,i)=>{
      const key=`hoja_HAPPY_IDLE_${i}`;
      if(!this.textures.exists(key))
        this.textures.addCanvas(key,makeHojaExpressionCanvas(fr,'happy'));
    });
    RAW_GROUPS.JUMP.forEach((fr,i)=>{
      const key=`hoja_HAPPY_JUMP_${i}`;
      if(!this.textures.exists(key))
        this.textures.addCanvas(key,makeHojaExpressionCanvas(fr,'happy'));
    });
  }


  createHojaAnimations(){
    // Animations are global to the game; Scene.restart must not recreate them.
    if(this.anims.exists('hoja-idle'))return;
    const mk=(key,group,rate)=>this.anims.create({
      key,frames:RAW_GROUPS[group].map((_,i)=>({key:`hoja_${group}_${i}`})),
      frameRate:rate,repeat:-1
    });
    mk('hoja-idle','IDLE',6);mk('hoja-walk','WALK',12);mk('hoja-run','RUN',14);
    mk('hoja-sleep','SLEEP',1); // 2 distinct 64x64 frames, 1 frame/second
    this.anims.create({
      key:'hoja-happy-idle',
      frames:RAW_GROUPS.IDLE.map((_,i)=>({key:`hoja_HAPPY_IDLE_${i}`})),
      frameRate:6,
      repeat:-1
    });
  }


  createBackground(){
    if(STAGE.theme==='cave'){
      if(this.caveBackground)this.caveBackground.destroy();
      if(this.bgLayers)for(const layer of this.bgLayers)layer.sprite.destroy();
      this.bgLayers=null;
      this.caveBackground=this.add.tileSprite(0,0,CAVE_SIDE,CAVE_SIDE,'caveBg')
        .setOrigin(0,0).setDepth(-49).setTint(HOJA_CAVE_BG_TINT);
      this.caveBackground.setTileScale(1,1);
      this.bgLayerScale=1;
      this.bgBaseY=VIEW_H/2;
      this.bgInitialScrollY=0;
      return;
    }
    if(this.bgLayers){
      for(const layer of this.bgLayers)layer.sprite.destroy();
    }

    const tex=this.textures.get(THEME.bg[0].key).getSourceImage();
    const srcH=tex.height||208;

    // Dynamic cover:
    // - minimum gameplay zoom is 0.90
    // - reserve extra area for vertical parallax and resize transitions
    // The full authored 288x208 coordinate system is scaled uniformly.
    const zoomReserve=VIEW_H/0.90;
    const verticalReserve=Math.max(96,VIEW_H*0.18);
    this.bgCoverHeight=Math.ceil(zoomReserve+verticalReserve);
    this.bgCoverWidth=Math.ceil(VIEW_W/0.90+Math.max(240,VIEW_W*0.22));
    this.bgLayerScale=this.bgCoverHeight/srcH;
    this.bgBaseY=VIEW_H/2;
    this.bgParallaxLimit=Math.max(42,Math.min(72,(this.bgCoverHeight-VIEW_H)*0.28));
    this.bgInitialScrollY=HOJA_MAKER_MODE?Math.max(0,STAGE.makerSpawnY-VIEW_H*.68):Math.max(0,Math.min(Math.max(0,SURFACE_VIEW_BOTTOM-VIEW_H),F-VIEW_H*0.68));

    const specs=THEME.bg.map((l,i)=>({...l,depth:-50+i*4}));

    this.bgLayers=specs.map(spec=>{
      const sprite=this.add.tileSprite(
        VIEW_W*RS/2,
        VIEW_H*RS/2,
        this.bgCoverWidth,
        this.bgCoverHeight,
        spec.key
      );

      sprite
        .setOrigin(.5,.5)
        .setScrollFactor(0,0)
        .setDepth(spec.depth)
        .setTileScale(this.bgLayerScale,this.bgLayerScale);

      sprite.tilePositionY=0;

      return {
        sprite,
        xFactor:spec.xFactor,
        yFactor:spec.yFactor,
        anchorTop:!!spec.anchorTop,
        key:spec.key,verticalOffset:spec.verticalOffset
      };
    });
    this.applyLightBackground();
    this.lastPaletteBand=-1; // new TileSprites need the active time palette
  }


  hojaV2DrawSolid(s,isCave){
    if(!Number.isFinite(s.x)||!Number.isFinite(s.y)||s.w<=0||s.h<=0)return;
    const sheet=this.textures.get('paV2Terrain');
    const original=sheet?.getSourceImage();
    if(!original)throw new Error('PA1 terrain image unavailable');
    const cols=Math.round(s.w/TILE),rows=Math.round(s.h/TILE);
    if(!cols||!rows)return;
    // One completed image per solid when short. Very wide ground is segmented
    // ONLY on 16px tile borders, never on a 256px source-chunk boundary.
    const n=64; // max 1024px width, a single raster per short/medium platform
    const full=!isCave&&s.y+s.h>=WORLD_H-1;
    const last=rows-1;
    const touches=(side,rowY)=>{
      if(s.kind!=='ground')return false;
      for(const o of SOLIDS){
        if(o===s||o.kind!=='ground')continue;
        const contact=side==='left'
          ? o.x<s.x-.01&&o.x+o.w>=s.x-.01
          : o.x+o.w>s.x+s.w+.01&&o.x<=s.x+s.w+.01;
        if(contact&&o.y<=rowY&&rowY<o.y+o.h)return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    const leftWater=WATER_SPANS.some(sp=>Math.abs(sp[1]*TILE-s.x)<.01);
    const rightWater=WATER_SPANS.some(sp=>Math.abs(sp[0]*TILE-(s.x+s.w))<.01);
    const tint=isCave?HOJA_CAVE_AMBIENT_TINT:0xffffff;
    const caveSolidFamily=isCave?hojaV2CaveSolidFamily(s):null;
    for(let start=0;start<cols;start+=n){
      const count=Math.min(n,cols-start);
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width=count*TILE;canvas.height=rows*TILE;
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
      if(!ctx)throw new Error('V2 terrain canvas unavailable');
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      for(let yy=0;yy<rows;yy++){
        for(let ii=0;ii<count;ii++){
          const xx=start+ii,worldX=s.x+xx*TILE,rowY=s.y+yy*TILE;
          const leftEdge=xx===0&&!(leftWater||touches('left',rowY));
          const rightEdge=xx===cols-1&&!(rightWater||touches('right',rowY));
          const tileColumn=leftEdge?0:(rightEdge?2:1);
          const tileRow=yy===0?0:(!full&&yy===last?2:1);
          const family=isCave?caveSolidFamily:(HOJA_V2_SURFACE_START[STAGE.theme]??0);
          const cutX=((isCave?0:6)+tileColumn)*TILE;
          const cutY=(family+tileRow)*TILE;
          ctx.drawImage(original,cutX,cutY,TILE,TILE,ii*TILE,yy*TILE,TILE,TILE);
        }
      }
      const key='hojaV2Solid_'+STAGE_INDEX+'_'+(this.hojaV2Serial++)+'_'+start;
      const tex=this.textures.addCanvas(key,canvas);
      if(!tex)throw new Error('V2 solid texture creation failed: '+key);
      tex.source?.[0]?.setWrap?.(Phaser.Textures.WrapMode.CLAMP_TO_EDGE,Phaser.Textures.WrapMode.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      const im=this.add.image(s.x+start*TILE,s.y,key).setOrigin(0,0).setDepth(0).setTint(tint);
      im.__hojaV2Solid=true;
      if(isCave){(this.hojaV2CaveArt||(this.hojaV2CaveArt=[])).push(im);}
    }
  }


  hojaV2DeleteStaleTextures(){
    for(const k of this.textures.getTextureKeys()){
      if(k.startsWith('hojaV2Solid_')||k.startsWith('hojaV2Decor_')||k.startsWith('hojaV2Cover_')||k.startsWith('hojaBankOcclusion_'))this.textures.remove(k);
    }
    this.hojaV2Serial=0;
  }


  emitDust(x,y,count=4,spread=24){
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const p=this.add.rectangle(
        x+(Math.random()-.5)*spread,
        y-2,
        2+(i%2),
        2+(i%2),
        0xfff8e6,
        .88
      ).setDepth(40);
      const vx=(Math.random()-.5)*26,vy=-(18+Math.random()*35);
      this.tweens.add({
        targets:p,
        x:p.x+vx,
        y:p.y+vy+18,
        alpha:0,
        scaleX:.4,scaleY:.4,
        duration:220+Math.random()*120,
        ease:'Quad.Out',
        onComplete:()=>p.destroy()
      });
    }
  }


  emitBurst(x,y,count=6,color=0xfff2b3,biasY=0){
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const side=i%2===0?-1:1;
      const p=this.add.rectangle(x+side*(2+(i%3)*2),y+(i%2)*2,3,3,color,1).setDepth(45);
      const dx=side*(18+(i%3)*11),dy=(-34+(i%4)*10)+biasY*20;
      this.tweens.add({
        targets:p,x:p.x+dx,y:p.y+dy,alpha:0,angle:90*side,
        duration:150+(i%3)*35,ease:'Quad.Out',
        onComplete:()=>p.destroy()
      });
    }
  }


  emitShockwave(x,y,radius){
    const ring=this.add.circle(x,y,7,0xffffff,0)
      .setStrokeStyle(2,0xfff3b0,.85).setDepth(39);
    const scale=Math.max(1,radius/7);
    this.tweens.add({
      targets:ring,scaleX:scale,scaleY:scale,alpha:0,
      duration:260,ease:'Quad.Out',onComplete:()=>ring.destroy()
    });
  }


  emitPoof(x,y,big=false){
    const n=big?10:8;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/n+Math.random()*.3;
      const r=(big?26:18)+Math.random()*10;
      const p=this.add.rectangle(x,y,4,4,i%2?0xffffff:0xfff1c9,1).setDepth(46);
      this.tweens.add({targets:p,x:x+Math.cos(a)*r,y:y+Math.sin(a)*r*.7-6,scaleX:.2,scaleY:.2,
        alpha:0,duration:260+Math.random()*80,ease:'Quad.Out',onComplete:()=>p.destroy()});
    }
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const st=this.add.rectangle(x,y,5,5,0xffe066,1).setAngle(45).setDepth(47);
      const dx=(i-1)*22,dy=-22-Math.random()*12;
      this.tweens.add({targets:st,x:x+dx,y:y+dy,angle:225,alpha:0,duration:380,ease:'Quad.Out',
        onComplete:()=>st.destroy()});
    }
    const ring=this.add.circle(x,y,6,0xffffff,0).setStrokeStyle(2,0xffffff,.9).setDepth(45);
    this.tweens.add({targets:ring,scaleX:big?4.2:3.2,scaleY:big?3.2:2.4,alpha:0,duration:240,
      ease:'Quad.Out',onComplete:()=>ring.destroy()});
  }


  playEnemyDefeat(e,cause){
    const spr=e.sprite;
    spr.setAlpha(1);
    if(cause==='stomp'){
      // Squashed flat under Hoja's feet, then pops into a puff.
      spr.setScale(2.7,1.0);
      this.tweens.add({targets:spr,scaleX:3.0,scaleY:.45,duration:90,ease:'Quad.Out',
        onComplete:()=>{this.emitPoof(spr.x,spr.y-8);spr.setVisible(false).setScale(2);}});
      return;
    }
    // Shell / strong STOMP: knocked upside-down and spun off the screen.
    const dir=spr.x<this.playerBody.x?-1:1;
    const x0=spr.x,y0=spr.y,o={t:0};
    spr.setFlipY(true);
    this.emitPoof(x0,y0-20,true);
    this.tweens.add({targets:o,t:1,duration:780,
      onUpdate:()=>{const t=o.t;spr.setPosition(x0+dir*96*t,y0-80*t+360*t*t).setAngle(dir*220*t);},
      onComplete:()=>spr.setVisible(false).setFlipY(false).setAngle(0)});
  }


}

</script>
<script id="hojaV053MusicSession">
"use strict";
window.HOJA_AUDIO_CONTROL=(()=>{
 const bgm=document.getElementById('hojaBgm');
 const trackElements=[bgm,document.getElementById('hojaTrackMeadow'),document.getElementById('hojaTrackAfternoon')];
 const names=['기존 음악 · 64kbps','HOJA Meadow','HOJA Afternoon'];
 const trackSources=trackElements.map(el=>el.getAttribute('src'));
 let trackIndex=-1,musicOn=true,sfxOn=true,unlocked=false;
 const frame=()=>document.getElementById('hqArcadeFrame');
 function render(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.hojaMusicToggle').forEach(el=>{el.textContent='♫ 음악';el.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(musicOn));el.title=(musicOn?'음악 켜짐 · 클릭하여 끄기':'음악 꺼짐 · 클릭하여 켜기')+' · '+(trackIndex<0?'인트로에서 선택':names[trackIndex]);});
  document.querySelectorAll('.hojaSfxToggle').forEach(el=>{el.textContent='♪ 효과음';el.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(sfxOn));el.title=sfxOn?'효과음 켜짐 · 클릭하여 끄기':'효과음 꺼짐 · 클릭하여 켜기';});
  const old=document.getElementById('soundBtn');if(old){old.textContent='♫ 음악';old.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(musicOn));old.title=trackIndex<0?'음악 선택 전':names[trackIndex];}
 }
 function syncArcade(){
  const f=frame();
  if(f?.contentWindow)try{f.contentWindow.postMessage({type:'HOJA_PARENT_AUDIO_STATE_V053',sfxOn:!!sfxOn},'*');}catch(_){}
  // Tutorial consumes the very same common music/SFX controls, including changes mid-session.
  const tf=document.getElementById('hojaTutApp');
  if(tf?.contentWindow&&!document.getElementById('hojaTutOverlay')?.hidden)
    try{tf.contentWindow.postMessage({kind:'HOJA_TUTORIAL_AUDIO_STATE',sfxOn:!!sfxOn,musicOn:!!musicOn},'*');}catch(_){}
 }
 function chooseNewRun(){
  trackIndex=Math.floor(Math.random()*3);
  const source=trackSources[trackIndex];
  if(source && bgm){
   if(bgm.getAttribute('src')!==source)bgm.src=source;
   bgm.loop=true;bgm.volume=.24;bgm.playbackRate=1;
   try{bgm.currentTime=0;}catch(_){}
   if(musicOn&&unlocked)bgm.play().catch(()=>{});
   else bgm.pause();
  }
  render();return trackIndex;
 }
 function unlockFromGesture(){
  unlocked=true;
  if(trackIndex<0)chooseNewRun();
  if(sfxOn)window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO?.enable?.();
  if(musicOn){bgm.volume=.24;bgm.play().catch(e=>console.warn('BGM playback blocked',e));}
  render();syncArcade();
 }
 function setMusic(on){
  musicOn=Boolean(on);
  if(musicOn){unlocked=true;if(trackIndex<0)chooseNewRun();bgm.volume=.24;bgm.play().catch(()=>{});}
  else bgm.pause();
  render();syncArcade();
 }
 function setSfx(on){
  sfxOn=Boolean(on);
  if(sfxOn){unlocked=true;window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO?.enable?.();}
  else window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO?.disable?.();
  render();syncArcade();
   if(!sfxOn)window.HOJA_TUTORIAL_AUDIO_BRIDGE?.stopSnore();
 }
 function toggleMusic(){setMusic(!musicOn)}
 function toggleSfx(){setSfx(!sfxOn)}
 document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const music=e.target.closest('.hojaMusicToggle');const sfx=e.target.closest('.hojaSfxToggle');
  if(!music&&!sfx)return;
  e.stopPropagation();
  if(music)toggleMusic();else toggleSfx();
 },true);
 window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',render);
 frame()?.addEventListener('load',syncArcade);
 return {chooseNewRun,unlockFromGesture,setMusic,setSfx,toggleMusic,toggleSfx,syncArcade,render,get musicOn(){return musicOn},get sfxOn(){return sfxOn},get selectedTrack(){return trackIndex},get unlocked(){return unlocked},get bgm(){return bgm}};
})();
</script>
<script>
"use strict";
// Real STEP13-7 embedded samples (verified by SHA-256), no procedural synthesis.
// Audio playback is unlocked only by a user gesture, as browsers require.
window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO=(()=>{
 const names=["step_grass1", "step_grass2", "collect", "hop", "owlrise", "jumpy", "roach", "bonk", "land", "strongstart", "strong", "shell1", "stomp1", "stomp2", "stagestart", "alert", "clear"];
 const refs=Object.fromEntries(names.map(k=>[k,document.getElementById('sfx_'+k)]));
 let enabled=false,ctx=null,master=null,loading=null;
 const buffers=Object.create(null),lastAt=Object.create(null);
 const Ctx=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
 const decode=(ctx,uri)=>{
  const bin=atob(uri.slice(uri.indexOf(',')+1)),v=new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)v[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return ctx.decodeAudioData(v.buffer);
 };
 async function load(){
  if(loading)return loading;
  loading=(async()=>{
   if(!Ctx)return;
   ctx=new Ctx();master=ctx.createGain();master.gain.value=.78;master.connect(ctx.destination);
   await ctx.resume();
   await Promise.all(names.map(async k=>{try{
    const el=refs[k];if(!el)return;
    let uri=el.getAttribute('src');
    if(!uri.startsWith('data:')){
     const r=await fetch(uri);if(!r.ok)throw Error('SFX fetch '+r.status);
     buffers[k]=await ctx.decodeAudioData(await r.arrayBuffer());
    } else buffers[k]=await decode(ctx,uri);
   }catch(e){console.warn('source SFX decode failed',k,e);}}));
  })();
  return loading;
 }
 function play(k,volume=.4,rate=1,gap=55){
  if(!enabled)return false;
  const now=performance.now();if(now-(lastAt[k]||-1e9)<gap)return false;lastAt[k]=now;
  const vol=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,volume)),speed=Math.max(.5,Math.min(2,rate));
  try{
   if(ctx&&ctx.state==='running'&&buffers[k]){
    const src=ctx.createBufferSource();src.buffer=buffers[k];src.playbackRate.value=speed;
    const gain=ctx.createGain();gain.gain.value=vol;src.connect(gain).connect(master);src.start();return true;
   }
   const orig=refs[k];if(!orig)return false;
   const el=orig.cloneNode(true);el.volume=vol*.78;el.playbackRate=speed;
   el.play().catch(()=>{});el.addEventListener('ended',()=>el.remove(),{once:true});return true;
  }catch(e){return false;}
 }
 function renderButton(){window.HOJA_AUDIO_CONTROL?.render?.();}
 function enable(){
  if(enabled)return;
  enabled=true;renderButton();
  load().then(()=>ctx?.resume?.()).catch(e=>console.warn('Audio unlock issue',e));
 }
 function disable(){
  enabled=false;renderButton();
 }
 function toggle(){if(enabled)disable();else enable();}
 document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',renderButton);
 return {play,enable,disable,toggle,get enabled(){return enabled}};
})();

</script>
<script>
"use strict";
// Only the choreography is intro-specific. Sprite textures, frame rates, terrain
// rasterizer, parallax constructor and particles are inherited from STEP13-7.
// Intro uses a uniform integer 2x camera zoom in BOTH phases.
// STEP13-7 Hoja/monster/terrain source geometry stays byte-identical.
// Pixel invariant: every 64x64 character texel maps to a square 2x2 display pixel.
// Camera zoom and character sprite scale are INTEGER and identical on both axes.
// In particular never use 1.5x camera zoom for the 64x64 character art.
let ATTRACT_W=960;
const ATTRACT_H=540, ATTRACT_TOTAL=35;
function hojaIntroMeasureWidth(){
 const rect=document.getElementById('frame')?.getBoundingClientRect();
 if(!rect||rect.width<=0||rect.height<=0)return 960;
 return Math.max(540,Math.min(2200,Math.round(ATTRACT_H*rect.width/rect.height)));
}
const PHASE1_ZOOM=2, PHASE2_ZOOM=2; // uniformly scaled square source pixels in BOTH phases
const CHASE_SPEED=270, CHASE_CAMERA_LEAD=280, NAHAN_FINAL_WORLD_X=1990;
// Chase world-frame camera width becomes 480 at 2x; a 280px lead keeps Hoja in view.
// Nahan at 1990 enters from the right ~3sec after the chase starts.
// Scene timings: Nahan passes first; monsters follow from THE SAME LEFT entrance.
// Intro-only timeline, not a modification of original STEP13-7 scene physics.
const BEATS=Object.freeze({
 stopStart:3.5,stopEnd:4.75,flower:8.2,nahanRush:10.0,nahanGone:12.75,
 monsters:13.05,harass:16.15,stomp:18.60,impact:19.02,
 heroExit:19.23,black:21.18,chase:21.62,
 nahanChaseShow:24.50,logo:29.10
});
const GROUNDLINE_FRACTION=0.79; // Keep the ground near its previous screen row when zooming out.
const HERO_FOOT=F, FLOWER_X=595, LOCK_CAMERA_LEFT=255;
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
const ease=t=>{t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t)};
class HojaAttractScene extends HojaSharedScene {
 init(data){this.elapsedMs=(data && Number(data.seek)||0)*1000;this.pausedByUser=false;this.stompTriggered=false;this.lastFootFx=-1;}
 create(){
  // Season is settled BEFORE the Phaser scene registers textures, background or V2 ground.
  this.introSeason=applyIntroSeason(window.HOJA_SEASON_SESSION.current());
  document.getElementById('frame')?.setAttribute('data-intro-season',this.introSeason);
  // Exact STEP13-7 registration and animator code. Source ASCII remains 64x64.
  const assetIds=[...Object.keys(HOJA_ASSET_MAP)];
  for(const id of assetIds){
   const item=HOJA_ASSET_MAP[id],el=document.getElementById(id);
   if(!el || !el.naturalWidth)throw new Error('Game art not loaded: '+id);
   if(this.textures.exists(item.key))continue;
   if(item.sheet)this.textures.addSpriteSheet(item.key,el,{frameWidth:item.sheet[0],frameHeight:item.sheet[1]});
   else this.textures.addImage(item.key,el);
  }
  // Register actual STEP13-7 four-season parallax layers once; these are
  // native 288x208 source images, never independent re-painted approximations.
  for(const id of [...new Set(Object.values(INTRO_SEASON_THEMES).flatMap(x=>x.bg.map(layer=>layer.key)))]){
    if(this.textures.exists(id))continue;
    if(Object.values(HOJA_ASSET_MAP).some(x=>x.key===id))continue;
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el?.naturalWidth)throw new Error('HOJA season image missing: '+id);
    this.textures.addImage(id,el);
  }
  // Exactly five Kenney Emotes (Pixel Style 3, original 16x16 PNG) as
  // intro-only textures; no runtime fetch, no other Kenney asset included.
  for(const mood of ['question','music','sweat','alarm','heart']){
   const tag=document.getElementById('kenney_emote_'+mood);
   if(!tag||!tag.naturalWidth||!tag.naturalHeight)throw new Error('Kenney emote failed: '+mood);
   const key='hoja_kenney_'+mood;
   if(!this.textures.exists(key))this.textures.addImage(key,tag);
  }
  this.registerHojaTextures();
  this.createHojaAnimations();
  this.cameras.main.setZoom(this.phaseZoom(this.elapsedMs/1000));
  this.cameras.main.roundPixels=true;
  const introSkyColors={spring:'#51b8ec',summer:'#51b8ec',autumn:'#fff3d8',winter:'#fff8ff'};
  this.cameras.main.setBackgroundColor(introSkyColors[this.introSeason]);
  this.createBackground();
  // Responsive intro: all seasonal TileSprites cover the widened logical viewport.
  // Keep the vertical origin untouched (same terrain / sea baseline and pixels).
  if(this.bgLayers){
   for(const layer of this.bgLayers){
    layer.sprite.setPosition(ATTRACT_W/2,layer.sprite.y);
    layer.sprite.setSize(Math.max(layer.sprite.width,ATTRACT_W+480),layer.sprite.height);
   }
  }
  // The STEP13-7 V2 terrain rasterizer receives a real authored solid (no V0.2 tiles).
  this.hojaV2DeleteStaleTextures();
  // Extend the actual source-V2 solid on exact 16px tile boundaries to cover
  // EVERY camera-visible point of the now longer pursuit and logo scene.
  // No painted filler, duplicated CSS strip, or extension of production files.
  let farthestCameraRight=0;
  for(let t=0;t<=ATTRACT_TOTAL+.001;t+=.05){
   farthestCameraRight=Math.max(farthestCameraRight,this.cameraLeft(t,this.heroAt(t))+ATTRACT_W/this.phaseZoom(t));
  }
  const neededTerrainW=Math.ceil((farthestCameraRight+128)/TILE)*TILE;
  SOLIDS[0].w=Math.max(SOLIDS[0].w,neededTerrainW);
  this.attractTerrainEnd=SOLIDS[0].x+SOLIDS[0].w;
  this.hojaV2DrawSolid(SOLIDS[0],false);
  this.hoja=this.add.sprite(250,HERO_FOOT,'hoja_IDLE_0').setOrigin(.5,61/64).setScale(1,1).setDepth(12).setFlipX(true);
  this.hoja.play('hoja-walk');
  // The supplied studio file contains 4 WALK and 6 HI original 64×64 frames.
  // They are already rasterized by registerHojaTextures() using the SAME source
  // palette, resolution and 61/64 bottom anchor as Hoja; no new PNG redraw.
  const mkNahan=(key,group,rate)=>{
   if(!RAW_GROUPS[group]||!RAW_GROUPS[group].length)throw new Error('Missing Nahan source group: '+group);
   if(!this.anims.exists(key))this.anims.create({key,
     frames:RAW_GROUPS[group].map((_,i)=>({key:`hoja_${group}_${i}`})),
     frameRate:rate,repeat:-1});
  };
  mkNahan('nahan-walk','NAHANSANG walk',14);
  mkNahan('nahan-hi','NAHANSANG Hi',8);
  this.nahan=this.add.sprite(-90,HERO_FOOT,'hoja_NAHANSANG walk_0')
    .setOrigin(.5,61/64).setScale(1,1).setDepth(14).setVisible(false);
  this.nahanPose='';
  this.playerBody={x:this.hoja.x}; // only used by the exact source defeat effect
  this.heroAnimName='hoja-walk';
  this.flower=this.add.sprite(FLOWER_X,HERO_FOOT+1,'flower_0').setOrigin(.5,1).setScale(1.3).setDepth(9);
  this.enemies=[
    this.enemy('JUMPY','jumpyIdle',125,'jumpy',false),
    this.enemy('HERMIE','hermieCrawl',70,'hermie',false),
    this.enemy('ROACH','roachRun',15,'roach',false),
    this.enemy('OWL','owlFly',-40,'owl',true),
    // STEP13-7's real 32x32 BLANKEY (4 frames); glides horizontally, never bobs.
    this.enemy('BLANKEY','blankeyFloat',-125,'blankey',true)
  ];
  // Original Kenney Pixel Style 3 texture, NOT custom Phaser geometry/text.
  // 16x16 source art * world scale 2 * integer 2x camera = square 4x4 display pixels.
  this.emoteSprite=this.add.image(0,0,'hoja_kenney_question')
    .setOrigin(.5,1).setScale(2,2).setDepth(90).setVisible(false);
  this.bubbleMood='';
  this.lastRumbleTick=-1;this.lastRumbleBurst=-1;
  // Original gameplay particles only, no synthetic blob overlay.
  // Large ellipse/drawn clouds removed by request. Original dust particles only.
  this.lastSoundTime=this.elapsedMs/1000;this.lastStepSound=-1;this.lastRumbleSound=-1;
  this.sweat=[];
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++)this.sweat.push(this.add.rectangle(0,0,3,5,0x39c5fa,1).setDepth(27).setVisible(false));
  this.overlay=this.add.rectangle(0,0,ATTRACT_W,ATTRACT_H,0x000000,1)
    .setOrigin(0,0).setScrollFactor(0).setDepth(200).setVisible(false);
  this.lastCameraLeft=0;
  this.lastCameraTop=HERO_FOOT-(ATTRACT_H/this.phaseZoom(this.elapsedMs/1000))*GROUNDLINE_FRACTION;
  if(window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2){
   this.elapsedMs=(BEATS.chase+.12)*1000;
   window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2=false;
  }
  this.drawAt(this.elapsedMs/1000,0,true);
  window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE=this;
  document.getElementById('status').textContent='Phaser · STEP 13-7 공통 렌더링';
 }
 enemy(type,texture,x,name,air){
  const n=this.textures.get(texture).frameTotal-1;
  const anim='hoja-attract-'+name;
  if(!this.anims.exists(anim)){
   this.anims.create({key:anim,frames:this.anims.generateFrameNumbers(texture,{start:0,end:Math.max(0,n-1)}),
     frameRate: name==='owl'?9:9,repeat:-1});
  }
  const sprite=this.add.sprite(x,HERO_FOOT,texture,0).setOrigin(.5,1).setScale(2).setDepth(11).setVisible(false);
  sprite.play(anim);
  return {type,texture,sprite,x,air,alive:true,initialX:x};
 }
 heroAt(t){
  let x=250,anim='hoja-walk',dy=0;
  if(t<BEATS.stopStart)x=250+47*t;
  else if(t<BEATS.stopEnd){
   // Cubic Hermite slowing: enter at the walking velocity, exit at zero.
   // This removes the abrupt stationary pose visible around 4.5 seconds.
   const u=(t-BEATS.stopStart)/(BEATS.stopEnd-BEATS.stopStart);
   const h00=2*u*u*u-3*u*u+1, h10=u*u*u-2*u*u+u;
   const h01=-2*u*u*u+3*u*u;
   x=h00*(250+47*BEATS.stopStart)+h10*(47*(BEATS.stopEnd-BEATS.stopStart))+h01*452;
  }else if(t<7.05){x=452;anim='hoja-idle';if(t>6.15&&t<6.90)dy=-Math.abs(Math.sin((t-6.15)*7))*6;}
  else if(t<BEATS.flower){x=lerp(452,540,ease((t-7.05)/1.15));anim='hoja-run';}
  else if(t<BEATS.stomp){x=540;anim=t>=10.9 && t<13.35?'hoja-idle':'hoja-happy-idle';
   if(t>=BEATS.harass)dy=-Math.abs(Math.sin(t*12))*3;}
  else if(t<BEATS.impact){x=540;anim='jump';const u=(t-BEATS.stomp)/(BEATS.impact-BEATS.stomp);dy=-52*Math.sin(Math.PI*u);}
  else if(t<BEATS.heroExit){x=540;anim='sit';}
  else if(t<BEATS.black){x=540+(t-BEATS.heroExit)*290;anim='hoja-run';}
  else {x=980+Math.max(0,t-BEATS.chase)*CHASE_SPEED;anim='hoja-run';}
  return {x,anim,dy};
 }
 cameraLeft(t,p){
  // Blend following -> fixed camera as Hoja coasts to a stop.
  if(t<BEATS.stopStart)return Math.max(0,p.x-235);
  if(t<5.05)return lerp(Math.max(0,250+47*BEATS.stopStart-235),LOCK_CAMERA_LEFT,ease((t-BEATS.stopStart)/1.55));
  if(t<BEATS.chase)return LOCK_CAMERA_LEFT;
  return Math.max(0,p.x-CHASE_CAMERA_LEAD);
 }
 phaseZoom(t){return t<BEATS.chase?PHASE1_ZOOM:PHASE2_ZOOM;}
 updateNahan(t){
  // First entrance: Nahan runs LEFT -> RIGHT, ahead of the monsters.
  // Final entrance: fixed world position; moving chase camera reveals him from
  // the RIGHT, then leaves him behind at the LEFT. He never slides in place.
  const first=t>=BEATS.nahanRush&&t<BEATS.nahanGone;
  const last=t>=BEATS.chase&&t<ATTRACT_TOTAL;
  const worldX=NAHAN_FINAL_WORLD_X;
  const visibleInFinal=last; // camera culls stationary Nahan naturally.
  if(!first&&!visibleInFinal){this.nahan.setVisible(false);return;}
  const pose=first?'nahan-walk':'nahan-hi';
  if(this.nahanPose!==pose){this.nahan.play(pose,true);this.nahanPose=pose;}
  if(first){
   this.nahan.setPosition(-45+(t-BEATS.nahanRush)*455,HERO_FOOT);
   this.nahan.setDepth(14).setFlipX(false);
  }else{
   // Fixed, grounded and facing the viewer; only the CAMERA crosses him.
   this.nahan.setPosition(worldX,HERO_FOOT);
   // Phase 2: strictly below Hoja/enemies but above rendered V2 ground (depth 0).
   this.nahan.setDepth(2).setFlipX(false);
  }
  const left=this.lastCameraLeft, right=left+ATTRACT_W/this.phaseZoom(t);
  this.nahan.setVisible(this.nahan.x>=left-32&&this.nahan.x<=right+32);
 }
 setHeroPose(p,t){
  this.hoja.setPosition(p.x,HERO_FOOT+p.dy).setFlipX(true);
  this.playerBody.x=p.x;
  if(p.anim==='jump'){
   const fr=clamp(Math.floor((t-BEATS.stomp)/(BEATS.impact-BEATS.stomp)*5),0,4);
   this.hoja.stop();this.hoja.setTexture('hoja_JUMP_'+fr);this.heroAnimName='jump';
  }else if(p.anim==='sit'){
   this.hoja.stop();this.hoja.setTexture('hoja_SIT_0');this.heroAnimName='sit';
  }else if(this.heroAnimName!==p.anim){
   this.hoja.play(p.anim,true);this.heroAnimName=p.anim;
  }
 }
 placeEnemies(t,p){
  const chase=t>=BEATS.chase;
  const present=t>=BEATS.monsters && t<BEATS.black;
  const harassment=t>=BEATS.harass&&t<BEATS.stomp;
  const crowd=ease((t-15.85)/.3);
  const approach=ease((t-BEATS.monsters)/2.65);
  const surround=[-68,70,-91,36,66],close=[-35,33,-18,12,38];
  for(let i=0;i<this.enemies.length;i++){
   const e=this.enemies[i],spr=e.sprite;
   if(!present&&!chase){spr.setVisible(false);continue;}
   if(present&&t>=BEATS.impact){
    if(!this.stompTriggered)spr.setVisible(false);
    continue; // copied STEP13-7 defeat tween owns positioning after impact
   }
   if(!e.alive&&!chase){spr.setVisible(false);continue;}
   let x=e.initialX,y=HERO_FOOT;
   if(chase){
    // Owl remains well behind Hoja; BLANKEY glides on a fixed horizontal lane.
    const trail=[40,80,120,160,200][i];
    x=p.x-trail+Math.sin(t*7+i*1.9)*3;
    y=e.type==='BLANKEY'?HERO_FOOT-110:(e.type==='OWL'?HERO_FOOT-95+Math.sin(t*7+i)*18:HERO_FOOT);
   }else{
    // ALL attackers enter behind Nahan, FROM THE LEFT; they then close in
    // around an unsuspecting Hoja and cover his silhouette at depth > 12.
    const target=p.x+lerp(surround[i],close[i],crowd);
    x=lerp(e.initialX,target,approach);
    if(e.type==='BLANKEY'){
     // BLANKEY glides without bobbing; smoothly descends toward Hoja's right flank as the crowd closes.
     y=HERO_FOOT-lerp(108,52,crowd);
    }else if(e.air){
     const altitude=lerp(104,55,crowd);
     y=HERO_FOOT-altitude+Math.sin(t*6.5+i)*7;
    }else if(harassment){
     const u=t-BEATS.harass;
     x+=Math.sin(u*13.5+i*1.85)*5;
     y=HERO_FOOT-Math.abs(Math.sin(u*10+i*1.4))*2;
    }
   }
   // Blue slime JUMPY now hops for its entire moving duration, even during
   // initial approach and the second chase. Ground contact repeats each cycle.
   if(e.type==='JUMPY'){
    const started=chase?BEATS.chase:BEATS.monsters;
    const phase=((t-started)*1.85)%1;
    y=HERO_FOOT-25*Math.max(0,Math.sin(Math.PI*phase));
   }
   spr.setDepth(harassment?17+i:11);
   if(chase){spr.setFlipY(false).setAngle(0).setScale(2).setAlpha(1);}   spr.setPosition(x,y).setFlipX(p.x<x).setVisible(true);
  }
 }
 triggerStomp(t,p){
  if(this.stompTriggered||t<BEATS.impact||t>=BEATS.impact+.15)return;
  this.stompTriggered=true;
  // These are VERBATIM inherited STEP13-7 methods, not a Canvas imitation.
  this.emitDust(p.x,HERO_FOOT,14,70);
  this.emitBurst(p.x,HERO_FOOT-6,18,0xfff3b0,1.0);
  this.emitShockwave(p.x,HERO_FOOT-2,94);
  this.cameras.main.shake(135,.008);
  for(const e of this.enemies){
   e.alive=false;
   e.sprite.setVisible(true);
   // Keep physical underside/flight coordinate; don't force the owl onto the ground.
   this.playEnemyDefeat(e,'strong');
  }
 }
 updateParallax(camLeft){
  if(!this.bgLayers)return;
  const zoom=this.phaseZoom(this.elapsedMs/1000), bgScreenScale=zoom, bgObjectScale=bgScreenScale/Math.max(.01,zoom);
  const inv=1/this.bgLayerScale;
  for(const layer of this.bgLayers){
   layer.sprite.tilePositionX=(camLeft*layer.xFactor)*inv;
   layer.sprite.setScale(bgObjectScale);
   // Keep clouds high but raise the actual mountain/forest artwork so it
   // appears ABOVE the grass surface. Offsets are in original 288x208 texels.
   // Changing tilePositionY never stretches individual source pixels.
   const verticalSourceOffsets={bgSky:0,bgCloud1:0,bgCloud2:12,bgHills:40,bgForeground:42};
   layer.sprite.tilePositionY=layer.verticalOffset??verticalSourceOffsets[layer.key]??0;
  }
 }
 // Intro-specific emotion cues using only the 5 bundled Kenney image textures.
 // Flower inspection alternates heart / music every 0.7 seconds.
 updateEmotionBubble(t,p){
  let mood='';
  if(t>=6.15&&t<7.08)mood='question';
  else if(t>=BEATS.flower&&t<11.28){
   mood=(Math.floor((t-BEATS.flower)/.7)%2===0)?'heart':'music';
  }
  else if(t>=11.30&&t<13.55)mood='question';
  else if(t>=13.55&&t<15.05)mood='alarm';
  else if(t>=BEATS.harass&&t<18.10)mood='sweat';
  else if(t>=18.10&&t<BEATS.stomp)mood='alarm';
  if(!mood){this.emoteSprite.setVisible(false);this.bubbleMood='';return;}
  if(this.bubbleMood!==mood){
   this.emoteSprite.setTexture('hoja_kenney_'+mood);
   this.bubbleMood=mood;
  }
  // Place the emote just above/right of Hoja's head. Uniform 2x world scale.
  // Source pixel edges stay crisp and characters are not rescaled.
  const bob=Math.round(Math.sin(t*3.2)*1.1);
  this.emoteSprite.setPosition(Math.round(p.x+22),Math.round(HERO_FOOT-82+p.dy+bob))
    .setVisible(true);
 }
 updateHarassDust(t,p){
  if(t<BEATS.harass||t>=BEATS.stomp)return;
  // The actual STEP13-7 particle functions; only the event frequency and
  // placement are scripted for this fast comedic melee.
  // More ORIGINAL STEP13-7 particles, not opaque ellipses or drawn cloud shapes.
  // Each puff is an independent short-lived particle; keep upper bodies legible.
  const tick=Math.floor((t-BEATS.harass)*1000/68);
  if(this.lastRumbleTick!==tick){
   this.lastRumbleTick=tick;
   const sway=Math.sin(t*13)*18;
   this.emitDust(p.x+sway,HERO_FOOT-2,8,43);
   this.emitDust(p.x-25-sway*.5,HERO_FOOT-9,5,25);
   this.emitDust(p.x+20+sway*.4,HERO_FOOT-13,5,23);
  }
  const burst=Math.floor((t-BEATS.harass)*1000/245);
  if(this.lastRumbleBurst!==burst){
   this.lastRumbleBurst=burst;
   this.emitBurst(p.x+Math.sin(t*13)*15,HERO_FOOT-24,4,0xf8edce,.1);
   if(burst%2===0)this.cameras.main.shake(60,.0014);
  }
 }
 updateSceneSfx(t,p){
  const previous=this.lastSoundTime??t;
  this.lastSoundTime=t;
  if(!window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO?.enabled)return;
  const cues=[
   [4.60,'land',.21,1.16], [6.22,'collect',.33,1.23],
   [8.25,'collect',.26,1.45], [10.02,'hop',.42,1.15],
   [11.33,'alert',.35,1.15], [13.07,'roach',.35,1.1],
   [13.63,'owlrise',.36,1.05], [15.0,'jumpy',.32,.98],
   [BEATS.stomp,'strongstart',.50,.92], [BEATS.impact,'strong',.72,.94],
   [BEATS.impact+.12,'stomp1',.48,1], [BEATS.heroExit,'hop',.35,1.2],
   [BEATS.chase+.06,'roach',.26,1.2], [BEATS.nahanChaseShow,'collect',.25,1.25],
   [BEATS.logo+.25,'clear',.38,1]
  ];
  // No catch-up noise on a seek/scene restart; only crossing current cues.
  for(const [at,key,vol,rate] of cues){
   if(previous<=at && t>at && t-previous<.22)window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO.play(key,vol,rate);
  }
  const stepRun=(t>0&&t<BEATS.stopStart)||(t>=7.05&&t<BEATS.flower)||(t>=BEATS.heroExit&&t<BEATS.black)||(t>=BEATS.chase);
  if(stepRun){
   const tick=Math.floor(t*1000/(t<BEATS.stopStart?285:205));
   if(tick!==this.lastStepSound){this.lastStepSound=tick;
    window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO.play(tick%2?'step_grass1':'step_grass2',.13,t<BEATS.stopStart?1:1.25);
   }
  }
  if(t>=BEATS.harass&&t<BEATS.stomp){
   const tick=Math.floor((t-BEATS.harass)*1000/260);
   if(tick!==this.lastRumbleSound){this.lastRumbleSound=tick;
    window.HOJA_INTRO_AUDIO.play(tick%3===0?'bonk':tick%2?'stomp2':'shell1',.38, .88+(tick%3)*.09);
   }
  }
 }
 updateFx(t,p,delta){
  const running=(t>=7.05&&t<BEATS.flower)||(t>=BEATS.heroExit&&t<BEATS.black)||(t>=BEATS.chase);
  if(running){
   const tick=Math.floor(t*1000/105);
   if(tick!==this.lastFootFx){
    this.lastFootFx=tick;
    this.emitDust(p.x-13,HERO_FOOT,2,12);
   }
  }
  this.updateEmotionBubble(t,p);
  this.updateHarassDust(t,p);
  this.updateSceneSfx(t,p);
  for(let i=0;i<this.sweat.length;i++){
   const el=this.sweat[i],visible=t>=BEATS.chase;
   const u=(t*2.2+i/3)%1;
   el.setPosition(p.x+24+i*4,HERO_FOOT-50+u*14).setAlpha(1-u).setVisible(visible);
  }
 }
 drawAt(t,delta,initial=false){
  const p=this.heroAt(t);
  this.setHeroPose(p,t);
  const zoom=this.phaseZoom(t),cx=this.cameraLeft(t,p),
        top=HERO_FOOT-(ATTRACT_H/zoom)*GROUNDLINE_FRACTION;
  this.cameras.main.setZoom(zoom);
  this.cameras.main.centerOn(cx+ATTRACT_W/(2*zoom),top+ATTRACT_H/(2*zoom));
  this.lastCameraLeft=cx;this.lastCameraTop=top;
  this.updateParallax(cx);
  this.updateNahan(t);
  // Fail visibly if later scene edits unintentionally introduce stretched art.
  if(Math.abs(this.hoja.scaleX-this.hoja.scaleY)>1e-8||Math.abs(this.nahan.scaleX-this.nahan.scaleY)>1e-8){
   throw new Error('CHARACTER_PIXEL_RATIO_INVALID');
  }
  // Bottom-center origin is planted at the same exact world Y on every frame.
  // A small 3-degree lean is a rooted flower swaying, never floating up/down.
  this.flower.setTexture('flower_'+(Math.floor(t*4)%2))
    .setPosition(FLOWER_X,HERO_FOOT+1).setAngle(Math.sin(t*2.25)*3);
  this.placeEnemies(t,p);
  if(t>=BEATS.impact && t<BEATS.impact+.13)this.triggerStomp(t,p);
  if(t>=BEATS.impact+.72 && !this.stompTriggered && t<BEATS.black)for(const e of this.enemies)e.sprite.setVisible(false);
  this.updateFx(t,p,delta);
  this.overlay.setVisible(false);
  document.getElementById('blackCut').style.opacity=(t>=BEATS.black&&t<BEATS.chase)?'1':'0';
  const logo=document.getElementById('hojaLogo');
  // User click-2 opens phase 2 and displays the sign immediately.
  const shortcutLogo=!!window.HOJA_INTRO_SHORTCUT_LOGO && t>=BEATS.chase;
  const showLogo=t>=BEATS.logo || shortcutLogo;
  const u=shortcutLogo?1:ease((t-BEATS.logo)/1.05);
  logo.style.opacity=showLogo?'1':'0';
  logo.style.transform=`translate(-50%, ${-530*(1-u)+u*25}px)`;
  document.getElementById('elapsed').textContent=t.toFixed(1)+' / '+ATTRACT_TOTAL.toFixed(0)+'초';
  document.getElementById('progress').style.width=(t/ATTRACT_TOTAL*100)+'%';
 }
 update(time,delta){
  if(this.pausedByUser)return;
  this.elapsedMs+=Math.min(60,delta);
  if(this.elapsedMs>=ATTRACT_TOTAL*1000){
   this.elapsedMs=ATTRACT_TOTAL*1000;
   this.pausedByUser=true;
   if(window.HOJA_REPOSITORY)window.HOJA_REPOSITORY.show();
   return;
  }
  this.drawAt(this.elapsedMs/1000,delta);
 }
}
// DOM embedded asset IDs mirror actual <img id=...> registration from Game.html.
const HOJA_ASSET_MAP={
 sgSky:{key:'bgSky'},sgCloud1:{key:'bgCloud1'},sgCloud2:{key:'bgCloud2'},
 sgHills:{key:'bgHills'},sgForeground:{key:'bgForeground'},
 paV2Terrain:{key:'paV2Terrain',sheet:[16,16]},
 enJumpyIdle:{key:'jumpyIdle',sheet:[32,32]},
 enHermieCrawl:{key:'hermieCrawl',sheet:[32,32]},
 enRoachRun:{key:'roachRun',sheet:[32,32]},
 enOwlFly:{key:'owlFly',sheet:[32,32]},
 enBlankeyFloat:{key:'blankeyFloat',sheet:[32,32]},
 enOwlIdle:{key:'owlIdle',sheet:[32,32]},
 flower_0:{key:'flower_0'},flower_1:{key:'flower_1'}
};
let attractGame=null;
async function bootAttract(){
 try{
  if(!window.Phaser)throw new Error('Phaser 엔진 다운로드 실패: 인터넷 연결을 확인하세요.');
  const images=[...document.querySelectorAll('img[data-hoja-asset],img[data-hoja-season-asset]')];
  await Promise.all(images.map(el=>el.decode?.()||Promise.resolve()));
  ATTRACT_W=hojaIntroMeasureWidth();
  attractGame=new Phaser.Game({
   type:Phaser.AUTO,parent:'phaserRoot',width:ATTRACT_W,height:ATTRACT_H,
   backgroundColor:'#51b8ec',pixelArt:true,roundPixels:true,
   render:{antialias:false,antialiasGL:false,roundPixels:true},
   scale:{mode:Phaser.Scale.FIT,autoCenter:Phaser.Scale.CENTER_BOTH,width:ATTRACT_W,height:ATTRACT_H},
   scene:[HojaAttractScene]
  });
  window.HOJA_ATTRACT_GAME=attractGame;
 }catch(err){
  document.getElementById('status').textContent='인트로 실행 오류';
  document.getElementById('status').style.color='#ffb9ae';
  const notice=document.getElementById('hojaIntroError');
  notice.textContent='호자 인트로 엔진을 불러오지 못했습니다. 인터넷 연결을 확인하고 페이지를 새로고침해 주세요.\n'+err.message;
  notice.style.whiteSpace='pre-line';notice.classList.add('visible');
  console.error(err);
 }
}
window.addEventListener('load',bootAttract);
window.HOJA_ATTRACT_CONTROL={
 restart(){window.HOJA_FLOW?.enterIntro?.();
  window.HOJA_INTRO_CLICKS?.reset();const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;if(!s)return;
  s.pausedByUser=false;s.anims.resumeAll?.();s.tweens.resumeAll?.();document.getElementById('pauseBtn').textContent='일시정지';s.scene.restart({seek:0});},
 seek(seconds){window.HOJA_FLOW?.enterIntro?.({newRun:false});window.HOJA_INTRO_CLICKS?.reset();
  const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;if(!s)return;
  s.pausedByUser=false;s.anims.resumeAll?.();s.tweens.resumeAll?.();document.getElementById('pauseBtn').textContent='일시정지';s.scene.restart({seek:Math.max(0,Math.min(ATTRACT_TOTAL-.1,seconds))});},
 togglePause(){const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;if(!s)return;
  s.pausedByUser=!s.pausedByUser;
  if(s.pausedByUser){s.anims.pauseAll?.();s.tweens.pauseAll?.();}else{s.anims.resumeAll?.();s.tweens.resumeAll?.();}
  document.getElementById('pauseBtn').textContent=s.pausedByUser?'이어보기':'일시정지';}
};

</script>

"use strict";
// TEST adapter. Uses the canonical 64x64 ASCII frames already registered in

const HOJA_LOADING=(function(){
 let ticker=0, startTime=0, active=false;
 const ids={root:'loadingOverlay',left:'loadingHoja',right:'loadingNahan',message:'loadingMessage',dots:'loadingDots'};
 function el(k){return document.getElementById(ids[k]||k);}
 function frames(group){
  const src=RAW_GROUPS[group]||[];
  if(!src.length)throw new Error('원본 JSON 애니메이션 없음: '+group);
  return src.map(frame=>makeHojaCanvas(frame).toDataURL('image/png'));
 }
 const hoja=frames('SLEEP'),nahan=frames('NAHANSANG Hi');
 function tick(){
   if(!active)return;
   const ms=performance.now()-startTime;
   // Square pixel aspect ratio guaranteed by 64x64 source and equal CSS width/height.
   el('left').src=hoja[Math.floor(ms/1000)%hoja.length];
   el('right').src=nahan[Math.floor(ms/125)%nahan.length];
   el('dots').textContent=['.','..','...','..'][Math.floor(ms/320)%4];
 }
 function show(options={}){
  hide();active=true;startTime=performance.now();
  el('message').textContent=HOJA_LOADING_MESSAGES[Math.floor(Math.random()*HOJA_LOADING_MESSAGES.length)];
  document.querySelector('.loadingNote').textContent=options.purpose==='questions'
    ?'선택한 단원의 문제 데이터를 불러오고 있습니다'
    :'카트리지 목록을 확인하고 있습니다';
  el('root').hidden=false;
  window.HOJA_CATALOG?.hide();
  // Same cadence for both frames, but animation source rates are independent.
  tick();ticker=setInterval(tick,125);
 }
 function hide(){
  active=false;if(ticker)clearInterval(ticker);ticker=0;
  el('root').hidden=true;
 }
 return {show,hide,messages:HOJA_LOADING_MESSAGES};
})();
window.HOJA_LOADING=HOJA_LOADING;

</script>
<script>
window.addEventListener('load',()=>{document.getElementById('logoL').src=document.getElementById('hojaPortrait').src;document.getElementById('logoR').src=document.getElementById('nahanPortrait').src});

"use strict";
window.HOJA_INTRO_SHORTCUT_LOGO=false;
window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2=false;
window.HOJA_INTRO_CLICKS=(()=>{
 let count=0;
 function reset(){count=0;window.HOJA_INTRO_SHORTCUT_LOGO=false;window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2=false;}
 function isPhase2(){const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;return !!window.HOJA_INTRO_SHORTCUT_LOGO||!!window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2||!!(s&&s.elapsedMs>=BEATS.chase*1000);}
 function toRepository(){count=3;const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;if(s){s.pausedByUser=true;s.anims.pauseAll?.();s.tweens.pauseAll?.();}window.HOJA_REPOSITORY?.show?.();}
 function advance(event){
  if(event?.target?.closest?.('#hojaRepoChoice,#hojaCatalog,#hojaNextMode,#hojaSharedBack,.hojaAudioTop'))return count;
  if(!document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').hidden||!document.getElementById('hojaRepoChoice').hidden||!document.getElementById('hojaCatalog').hidden||!document.getElementById('hojaNextMode').hidden)return count;
  // When PHASE 2 is already playing (naturally or via click-2), one click finishes intro.
  if(isPhase2()){toRepository();return count;}
  count=Math.min(count+1,3);
  if(count===1){window.HOJA_AUDIO_CONTROL?.unlockFromGesture?.();}
  else if(count===2){
   window.HOJA_INTRO_SHORTCUT_LOGO=true;
   const s=window.HOJA_ATTRACT_SCENE;
   if(s){s.pausedByUser=false;s.anims.resumeAll?.();s.tweens.resumeAll?.();document.getElementById('pauseBtn').textContent='일시정지';s.scene.restart({seek:BEATS.chase+.12});}
   else window.HOJA_INTRO_PENDING_PHASE2=true;
  }else if(count===3)toRepository();
  return count;
 }
 document.getElementById('frame')?.addEventListener('click',advance);
 return {advance,reset,isPhase2,get count(){return count}};
})();;

</script>
<script>
"use strict";
/* V0.5.8 direct Apps Script HtmlService adapter. Production Project B read-only.
 * Only the already audited server's public RPC methods are called. */
window.HOJA_B_LIVE=(()=>{
 const METHODS=['LIST','GET','RANK_LIST','RANK_SAVE'];
 let sources=null,names=[],serial=0,rankInFlight=0;
 const getFrame=()=>document.getElementById('hqArcadeFrame');
 const showError=e=>String(e?.message||e||'알 수 없는 서버 오류');
 function rpc(method,payload){
  if(!METHODS.includes(method))return Promise.reject(new Error('RPC_METHOD_DENIED'));
  const gs=window.google?.script?.run;
  if(!gs)return Promise.reject(new Error('Apps Script 웹앱 연결이 없습니다. 배포된 /exec 주소에서 실행해 주세요.'));
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   let finished=false;
   const timeout=window.setTimeout(()=>{if(!finished){finished=true;reject(new Error('서버 응답 시간 초과'))}},45000);
   const success=value=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;clearTimeout(timeout);resolve(value)};
   const failure=error=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;clearTimeout(timeout);reject(new Error(showError(error)))};
   const runner=gs.withSuccessHandler(success).withFailureHandler(failure);
   try{
    if(method==='LIST')runner.hojaListPublicCartridges();
    else if(method==='GET')runner.hojaGetPublicQuestions(payload);
    else if(method==='RANK_LIST')runner.hojaRankList(payload);
    else if(method==='RANK_SAVE')runner.hojaRankSave(payload);
   }catch(error){failure(error)}
  });
 }
 function setSources(values){
  if(!Array.isArray(values)||!values.length||values.length>5)throw new Error('카트리지 1~5개가 필요합니다.');
  if(values.some(e=>!e||typeof e.sheetName!=='string'||!Array.isArray(e.data?.questions)))throw new Error('Project B 응답이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  window.HOJA_GAMEPLAY?.setServerQuestions?.(values);
  sources=values;
  names=values.map(x=>x.sheetName);
 }
 function clear(){sources=null;names=[];window.HOJA_GAMEPLAY?.clearServerQuestions?.();}
 function sendFrame(obj){const f=getFrame();if(f?.contentWindow)f.contentWindow.postMessage(obj,'*')}
 window.addEventListener('message',async event=>{
  const f=getFrame();if(!f?.contentWindow||event.source!==f.contentWindow)return;
  const msg=event.data;if(!msg||typeof msg!=='object')return;
  if(msg.type==='HOJA_V058_ARCADE_READY'){
   if(sources)sendFrame({type:'HOJA_V058_ARCADE_START',sources,names});
   else sendFrame({type:'HOJA_V058_ARCADE_ERROR',error:'선택한 카트리지 문제가 없습니다.'});
   return;
  }
  if(msg.type!=='HOJA_V058_ARCADE_RPC')return;
  const id=msg.id,method=msg.method;
  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(id)||id<1||rankInFlight>=2)return;
  // Read/Save rankings only for the currently selected single public cartridge.
  if(names.length!==1||!['RANK_LIST','RANK_SAVE'].includes(method)||
      (method==='RANK_LIST'&&msg.payload!==names[0])||
      (method==='RANK_SAVE'&&msg.payload?.cartridge!==names[0])){
   sendFrame({type:'HOJA_V058_ARCADE_RPC_RESULT',id,ok:false,error:'랭킹은 단일 공용 카트리지의 정상 완주 기록만 지원합니다.'});
   return;
  }
  rankInFlight++;
  try{const value=await rpc(method,msg.payload);sendFrame({type:'HOJA_V058_ARCADE_RPC_RESULT',id,ok:true,value})}
  catch(error){sendFrame({type:'HOJA_V058_ARCADE_RPC_RESULT',id,ok:false,error:showError(error)})}
  finally{rankInFlight--}
 });
 return {rpc,setSources,clear,get sources(){return sources},get names(){return names.slice()},showError};
})();
</script>
